Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the lists object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 341. The moduli space has dimension at most 20 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_341(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 341. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^20(S, F) is trivial. QED.
